STRATEGY EXPLAINED:

Forex ke 5 minute chart pe jaao aur LuxAlgo ka ORB indicator lagao. Settings mein New York timezone (UTC-4) select karo. Indicator aapko opening range mark kar dega.
Step 1: New York open ke pehle 3 candles ka high aur low mark ho jaata hai — yahi ORB (Opening Range Breakout) hai.

Step 3: Uss displacement move mein do cheezein mark karo:
→ Demand Zone: woh aakhri red candle jiske baad displacement shuru hua
→ FVG (Fair Value Gap): displacement ke andar 3 candles ke beech jo gap banta hai
Step 4: Jab price pullback karke inn zones mein se kisi mein bhi aaye aur bada green candle bane — wahan BUY entry lo.
Step 5: Stoploss zone ke neeche. Target: 2R agar stoploss tight (FVG pullback), 1.5R agar stoploss wide (demand zone pullback). SELL ke liye bilkul ulta.

stoploss.

breakout kare.

tod de, tab turant BUY entry lo.

55 points tak hi SL lo. Stocks mein max 100 points.
 
Target — Fixed 1:4 risk reward. Matlab agar 40 points
ka SL hai toh 160 points ka target.
 
SELL Trades — Upper band ke upar same logic ka ulta.

Inside bar candle trading strategy logic:
We will open daily timeframe chart of a liquid stock on trading view, put EMA 10 indicator on it, and when the price moves above EMA 10 indicator, then we will find inside bar candle which is a candle which easily fits inside high and low of the previous candle, thus it is called inside bar candle and the larger bar is called Mother candle. And, as soon as the breakout happens on high of the mother candle, we take BUY trade, with stoploss a notch below low of the inside bar candle and target decided by risk reward ratio of 1:2 to 1:3.

And, we can use the reverse of this trading strategy logic to do short selling, if we were trading on Nifty 50 or any index.

Then here we will take the ATM option i.e. the strike price which is closest to the bitcoin spot price will be taken as our ATM strike price, and in that ATM strike price, we will add the bid value of both Call and Put options and get a straddle price, the sum value of this straddle price will be used as a range both in upward and downward level from the strike price on the bitcoin cryptocurrency chart, and this range will be our safety cushion.As long as the price is between this range, we are in profits, and whenever price breaks out of any level from either side, then again we will take a second straddle range on that breakout and we can do this range marking three times as per Pushkar Raj thakur, and that is why he calls it a triple straddle trading strategy for options selling on bitcoin which simply means that we can sell Call and Put options three times in this options selling trading strategy on bitcoin.

For more guidance, viewers can watch pushkar raj thakur video on this options selling strategy on youtube.

I have backtested this triple straddle options selling strategy of Pushkar Raj thakur for 1 year using python code and created an algo for this, took the backtesting data from delta exchange APIs.

=== [114] This Smart Money Concept based Fair Value Gap crypto trading strategy gives most profitable trades ===
Link: https://www.youtube.com/shorts/Erxc3a1c3jk

#neerajjoshi #cryptotradingstrategy #algotrading 
Smart Money Concept (SMC) based trading strategies are more profitable as these trading strategies are followed by institutions, smart money, Domestic and Foreign Institutional investors.

The same thing has been said and accepted by Neeraj Joshi, a share market trader and youtuber who has 5 million subscribers on youtube, in his Fair Value Gap (FVG) trading strategy video which is based on Smart Money Concept (SMC).

So, let's do the backtest of this strategy for 1 year like I have done for multiple other strategies and see if this FVG strategy of Neeraj Joshi is indeed profitable or not.

So, we will go yo delta exchange platform, here we will open the 1 hour timeframe chart of bitcoin (BTCUSD) cryptocurrency,and whenever we see any BIG bullish green candle on the chart, we will make a zone from the high of previous candle to the low of the next candle of this big green candle.The zone which will be created by this is called Fair Value Gap (FVG) zone.
After that, whenever the candle will retrace back to the middle line of this FVG zone i.e. at 50% of the zone, we will take the long position, with stoploss just below the FVG zone lowest level and target at recent swing high.The Risk to reward (R:R) ratio for FVG trading strategy can be put between 1:2 to 1:4.

The reverse of the strategy logic will be used for the short trades.

The backtest of this smart money trading strategy has been done on python code and the data I have taken from Delta Exchange platform.

This FVG crypto trading strategy of Neeraj Joshi has given an annual return of 73%, winning trades of 49%, drawdown of 21%, and the equity curve and PnL distribution graph has been shared in the reel.

Now, I will create the algo for this SMC-based crypto FVG intraday and swing strategy so that the trades can be executed automatically using AlgoHub option of Delta Exchange platform to create API.

So I had a genuine question in my mind —
does this 8 & 30 EMA intraday strategy of wizard trader really work in real market conditions,
or does it just look good on charts and videos?

That’s why I decided to backtest Wizard Trader’s 8–30 EMA intraday strategy using real data and Python code.

Wizard Trader’s 8–30 EMA trading strategy logic:
• Uses 8 EMA and 30 EMA for trend direction and momentum confirmation
• Enters the trade when price breaks both EMAs or when EMA crossover happens
• Stop-loss at recent swing high / low
• Risk–reward around 1:2
• Designed for intraday trading (long & short both)

I backtested this strategy using Python code and took historical market data from Dhan API, and here are the results.

My backtest results of Wizard Trader’s 8–30 EMA intraday trading strategy:
• ₹1 lakh → ₹46,840 profit
• Win rate: 57.92%
• Total trades: 312
• Max drawdown: 24.36%

So, the algo backtest results of Wizard Trader’s 8–30 EMA strategy were not bad at all.
It’s a simple trend-following intraday strategy, but like every strategy, risk management and market conditions matter a lot.

=== [153] Backtest of Abhishek kar simplest intraday strategy. ===
Link: https://www.youtube.com/shorts/op1d1FKIk0I

@abhishekkarofficial is a well-known stock market educator and trader, and according to him, the trading strategy shared in the reel is one of the simplest intraday trading strategies.

So, I had a genuine question in my mind --- Can simple trading strategies really make money in the market, or do we always need multiple indicators and comples setups like we saw in the previous video where I backtested @pushkarrajthakur Brahmastra Strategy and the results were quite shocking?

To find the answer, I decided to backtest Abhishek Kar simplest intraday trading strategy as mentioned in the reel using market historical data from the Dhan API.

This simple intraday trading strategy logic explained by Abhishek Kar in his youtube video is:

1.Trade on 5-minute timeframe
2.Wait 1-1.5 hours after market is open
3.Enter when price breaks the day's high or low with high volume spurt, which is an ORB strategy i em Opening Range Breakout strategy.
4.Use VWAP as confirmation.
5.Risk-Reward ratio: 1:1 or 1:2

I backtested this strategy using python code and took historical data from Dhan API, honestly, the results were quite unexpected.

My 1 -year backtest results of Abhishek kar simplest intraday trading strategy are:
•Total return: 34.26% i.e. 1 lakh rupees will become Rs.1,34,260 in 1 year.
•Win rate: 53.40%
•Total trades: 268
•Drawdown: 26.78%

So, based on one backtest, it's difficult to say that a Simple trading strategy will always work, because false breakouts and whipsaws are a real challenge in intraday trading.

That's why I want to backtest more simple trading strategies using data and code before making a conclusion whether simple trading strategies work or not in real stock market trades.

Comment and tell me which simple trading strategy should I backtest next?

=== [167] Salary Toh Aayi Par Gayi Kahan 😭💸 ===
Link: https://www.youtube.com/shorts/CtOnh8Tl1Zo

💸 **Salary credit hui... par gayi kahan bhai?!**

Har ek naukri wale ka dard hai yeh – yeh **hilarious aur eye-opening short** dikhata hai kaise **salary aate hi khushi hoti hai, par kuch dino mein budgeting ka horror ban jaata hai.**

Truffle pasta se leke OTT renewals, sneakers se leke impulse buys tak — yeh funny sketch dikhata hai aaj ke “*treat yourself*” culture mein **income, expenses aur budgeting** ka asli struggle.
